- **Endowment Effect**: People value things more highly simply because they own them; willingness to accept is typically 2-3x higher than willingness to pay ([lev-os/agents](https://github.com/lev-os/agents/tree/HEAD/skills-db/thinking/patterns/endowment-effect) · ★27)
- **endowment-effect**: Activate when: seller is asking way more than buyers will pay; a founder or homeowner insists their asset is worth far more than market comps; a team refuses to cut a feature they built; a free-trial cancellation flow has more friction than signup; someone says 'I've put too much into this to sell for that.' Do NOT activate when: the valuation gap is explained by genuine information asymmetry the seller actually has; the asset is a pure commodity with a transparent live market price (endowment effect is weak when reference prices are salient). More: deciqai.com/s/endowment-effect ([deciqAI/knowledge-skills](https://github.com/deciqAI/knowledge-skills/tree/HEAD/endowment-effect) · ★16)

Use quando a pessoa disser que vive cansada, que trabalha muito e não rende, que não tem tempo ou que a tarde não rende. Também é a base do /energia. ([codetradeai/oficio](https://github.com/codetradeai/oficio/tree/HEAD/skills/energia-e-tempo) · ★2)
- **energia-eletrica-recuperacao-consumo**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Energia Elétrica: Recuperação de Consumo (Tema 699 STJ). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/consumidor/energia-eletrica-recuperacao-consumo))
- **energia-reclami**: Write energy complaints with ARERA desk path. Use when asked reclamo energia, luce gas complaint, sportello ARERA. ([terzastella/italy-work-skills](https://github.com/terzastella/italy-work-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/energia-reclami) · ★2)
- **energia-solar-consumidor-geracao-distribuida**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Energia Solar e Geração Distribuída: Consumidor (Lei 14.300/2022). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/consumidor/energia-solar-consumidor-geracao-distribuida))
- **energia-solar-fotovoltaica**: Dimensionado de sistemas solares fotovoltaicos en Argentina, aislados (off-grid) y conectados a la red (generación distribuida), con memoria de cálculo: horas de sol pico (HSP) del Atlas solar argentino, PVGIS, NASA POWER o Global Solar Atlas, tabla de consumos (Wh/día y potencia simultánea), cantidad de paneles con pérdidas desglosadas, conexión en serie y en paralelo con corrección de Voc y Vmp por temperatura (NOCT), regulador PWM o MPPT, banco de baterías (autonomía, profundidad de descarga, 12, 24 o 48 V, plomo-ácido o LiFePO4), inversor (potencia continua y arranque de motores), cables,… ([lagzgabrielmuller-dot/kit-ingenieria-3d](https://github.com/lagzgabrielmuller-dot/kit-ingenieria-3d/tree/HEAD/plugins/ingenieria-3d/skills/energia-solar-fotovoltaica))
